- **inv-callstack**: ある関数/モジュールを「誰が呼んでいるか」を呼び出し元へ遡って木構造で示す手順。責務の置き場所を決めたい・引数や API を消せるか知りたい・変更の影響範囲を測りたいときに使う。順方向(何を呼ぶか)は /callstack で、図の形は共通 ([Mikanixonable/dive-into-tepui](https://github.com/Mikanixonable/dive-into-tepui/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/inv-callstack))
- **inv-runner**: Python Invoke task automation for DTX website. This skill should be used when running repo commands, backend tests via Docker, database migrations, updating invoke tasks, Docker operations, or workflow automation. Supports dev/test/prod environments with run, exec, up, down commands. References docs/development/invoke-tasks.md for complete command reference. ([kettleofketchup/DraftForge](https://github.com/kettleofketchup/DraftForge/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/inv-runner) · ★15)
- **inv-think-partner**: 当行业出现重大变化、市场讨论热度发生显著转变、需要收窄投资标的池（从一批候选筛选核心+弹性标的）、判断行业贝塔方向（见顶/见底信号）、评估公司叙事变化（市场看法是否在转向）或对比不同标的确定性高低时使用。即使用户说'帮我理一下这几个标的怎么选'、'这个行业现在到哪了'、'市场对这家公司的看法变了吗'、'A和B哪个更确定'、'帮我收窄一下标的'、'挑3-4个重点标的'、'行业景气度怎么样'、'叙事变了没'，也应触发。 ([LuoWQ2025/AShare_Fundamental_Trend_Trading](https://github.com/LuoWQ2025/AShare_Fundamental_Trend_Trading/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/inv-think-partner) · ★2)
- **invade**: Set up a repository's knowledge base from what the repository can prove — explore it read-only in four facets (its domains, the truth it writes down, the truth its code enforces, its decisions and words), show one map and take every answer in one message, then write proposed register entries (an index entry pointing at each rule a page already states, a drafted entry for each truth only the code enforces), fill the playbook forms from evidence with every command run green, leave an honest TODO(human) question where proof is missing, with design craft on draft one screen of the screen library… ([vertuoza/vertuo-omni-loop](https://github.com/vertuoza/vertuo-omni-loop/tree/HEAD/kit/plugin/skills/invade) · ★1)
- **invaders**: Launch Clawd Invaders, a retro Space Invaders browser game starring Clawd the pixel crab. Use this skill when the user types /invaders, asks to play the bug game, wants to play an arcade game, mentions Clawd Invaders, or asks for an easter egg. ([avdm12/clawd-invaders](https://github.com/avdm12/clawd-invaders/tree/HEAD/skills/invaders))
- **invaders-arch**: Code structure rules for invaders's game (first-person Space Invaders: pure Logic features vs thin Roblox adapters, the same require rules as pickman's, server authority, and how it uses the arcade library).
